2026-10-25 00:41:17 | HANDS  | T209 try 1: FIX `eval --ambiguous` reports two accuracies (PLAN R5-12, R5-14 - every place the score shows: report.json score, report.md, console): `report._report` sets `score.total = len(results)` and `decision = _decision(len(results), passed)`, but `run_eval(..., ambiguous=...)` returns golden + ambiguous lines in one list, so an `--ambiguous` run prints `Skor: 35/47` and report.md opens with that score/decision while the metrics block printed right below reports `Doğruluk %87,50` (= pass/golden = 35/40). The ambiguous lines are meant to clarify, not pass, so folding them into the kill-gate score understates accuracy and can flip the decision bucket; the integration test's own comment ("belirsiz set altın cevabın yerine geçmez") says the score must equal a plain run's, and it does not. Fix: when ambiguous lines are present, `score.passed`/`score.total` (report._report) and the console `Skor:` line (cli.evaluate) count only `kind == "golden"` results, so the score equals the plain run's and matches `metrics.accuracy`; `metrics` and `questions` keep every line, and a plain run's score is unchanged

2026-10-26 13:11:01 | HANDS  | T346 try 1: phone nav says it scrolls (M21 review 1): at 390 the nav row is scrolled on purpose (overflow-x auto with scrollbar-width none and the webkit scrollbar hidden, web/src/styles.css:962-972) and the cut edge is meant to be the clue, which it is not: "Veritabanı haritası" prints as "Veritabanı harita", and because the row sits scrolled to the current page the Kullanıcılar frame opens starting at "Veritabanı haritası" with "Sor" and "Kurallar" off to the left and nothing to say they exist (users-screen-users-table-390.png, rules-screen-validation-390.png). Fix: keep the single scrolled row and give the overflowing side a real fade - a mask-image with its -webkit- twin drawn as a gradient going transparent at the inline-end edge of .masthead-nav in the phone block - so the clipped label reads as "continues this way", and keep .nav-link flex none so the fade is not paid for by wrapping. New tests/test_web_nav_affordance.py (reads web/src/styles.css the way tests/test_web_masthead_css.py does - comments stripped, only media blocks whose max-width reaches 390 - and asserts the winning .masthead-nav declarations include a mask-image or -webkit-mask-image whose value is a gradient, and that .nav-link still resolves to flex none)

So in English a fabricated scaled number reaches the user unchecked. Fix: in `lang="en"` the scale suffix is read as a scale - attached `K`/`M`/`B` (case-insensitive, only directly after the digits and not followed by another letter) and the words thousand/million/billion (also after a space) multiply the value (tolerance scales like the Turkish word); the Turkish path stays byte-identical (bin/milyon/milyar, `12K` still not a number in tr). Add tests/test_guard_en_scale.py (new file: allowed [12500.0] -> "12.5K" and "12.5 thousand" pass, "99.9K" and "99.9M" are offending, "3.4M" passes for 3400000, `STK001` and "Q3" still are not numbers, `format_compact(v, "en")` output always passes `check_text` for v in 12500, 3_400_000, -2_100_000_000, and the tr results for the same texts are unchanged)
